## What Volume 05 has now put on the record, added to Batch 0002's sixteen

17. **THE SHEET, AND THE THING THAT WAS NOT ON IT.** Four copies, one name at the top of each, two figures at the same kerb at the same hour thirty-five days apart, nine inches, a branch has to come off the core, the east one, the arithmetic reason, and the refusal to convert. **It does not say how many times the later figure was taken, and that is the whole of the volume's major turn.** He read the two figures out and read the five things that were NOT on the sheet out afterwards, in a yard, and counted the five, and refused to say which of the five was the one that mattered.
18. **THE VILLAGE CUT ITS OWN LAUNDER AND NOBODY ASKED IT.** Eleven people of Marden, two saws, a barrow each, a bucket with writing on the side of it, the fourth hour of the afternoon because the launder is lowest then. **A village gives up the thing it was already short of, and it gives it up because it has the least, and nobody asked it.** The answer to the volume's own question arrived by accident, in the wrong village, and the woman of about thirty-eight told him to write down what it means and not what it means about.
19. **THE DRAW THAT WAS NOT BEGUN.** Line one of a rule of four lines with no fifth line, read aloud by a licensed man in his own words, entered by him in his own book as a thing not begun and NOT as a thing stopped, and the reason for the difference given out loud: a draw that is not begun is a decision and a draw that is stopped is an act of a rule, and the district record is a record of acts of rules. **The count of the uses of the standing rule this season is one and it went up on a thing that was not done.**
20. **TWO HUNDRED AND THIRTY-ONE TREES, AND A MAN WHO SAID THE NUMBER OUT LOUD.** A true figure of a pressure and a false figure of a cause. The head fell nine inches at the west kerb and the west stand came into leaf. The north stand is in bud and is the control. **The man who finally said it was a smallholder of about fifty-eight who has been putting dead wood into that ground for eleven years, and he said he would like one person in this book to write down that it was for nothing, and there is not going to be anybody else who is going to say it. Nobody thanked him and he is not a villain and his refusal to be thanked for saying it is on the page.**
21. **A SHEET WITH TWO LINES UNDER THE FIGURE, THE SECOND HEADED NOTHING ELSE, SENT FOUR HUNDRED MILES TO A YARD OF FOUR HOUSEHOLDS.** Unsigned, unstamped, no instruction, fourpence. **She filled in one of the two lines and left the other empty on purpose and wrote under it that she is not going to say why.** The line she filled in is MEASURED TWICE. It is the first thing anybody in this book has ever sent that household in twelve years.
22. **THE SEVEN THAT DECIDE AT MARDEN LET A STONE DECIDE, AND ASKED FOR THE LINE TO BE ENTERED AS THAT, IN THOSE WORDS, WITH NO NAME IN IT.**

## The things a Volume 05 Batch 0004 writer must not mistake for open, or re-spend, or answer

**Do not answer whose order it is. Do not reduce the day-and-not-the-figure count of three. Do not move the use log off fifteen without a contact on the page. Do not move the count of pattern-holders for four off two and do not move the count for five off one. Do not add a fifth account of the arm, and do not enter the head that went on the twenty-third of the first month as the mark — it has been named twice in Batch 0003 as a REASON and both times entered as not a mark. Do not use the standing rule a second time; its one use this season was a thing that was NOT done. Do not spend the Fieldbook panel before the climax. Do not name the Crown Engine, Iona Vey, a Deep Archive, a caretaker link or a seedheart. Do not name a pulse and do not say a pulse came on a schedule. Do not close Garrow Nye's sequence, do not reconcile the four accounts of the arm, do not recover the vault, do not make the corridor a road. Do not thank anybody and do not write anything tender. Do not name a month. Do not resolve who speaks for the eleven acres of standing willow. Do not fill the six blank columns on the well house door. Do not write a decomposition of the four feet of shortfall. Do not write an entry for the well on the shelf coming back, do not supply the tenth figure, and do not carry the half inch forward as a figure. Do not let the forty hundredweight off the fifth launder become a fifth share. Do not put a name in the fourth line of the rota and do not let anybody answer the question of whose name goes in it.**

**AND FOUR THAT ARE SPECIFIC TO BATCH 0003 AND WILL NOT BE OBVIOUS.** **Do not put a second figure on the west kerb sill that disagrees with TWO FEET SEVEN INCHES on day 361 and ONE FOOT TEN INCHES on day 396 and ONE FOOT TEN INCHES AGAIN on day 404.** **Do not turn the count of persons who can hold a pattern for four into one or into three, and do not turn the count of five into two.** **Do not say that Marden was wrong to cut its launder, and do not let anybody say it was right, and do not let the east launder be healed, restored, re-opened or re-formed this season — the ground under that cut was NOT MEASURABLE because a draw was not begun, and that not known has been given and is out.** **And do not let the woman of about thirty-eight of Marden or the woman of about fifty-one be thanked, softened, or made into a lesson, and do not let the volume's own question be answered in a sentence by a man in a road: it has been answered by a village with two saws and that is the only answer it is going to get.** **AND ONE THAT WAS ADDED ON THE REVIEW FIX PASS BECAUSE THE NEXT-PHASE PROMPT DID NOT CARRY IT AND A WRITER WOULD HAVE HAD TO GUESS: the wall at Cauldron Reach holds TWELVE FIGURES AND ONE GAP, and the thirteenth figure is FIVE FEET and is on that wall now, and no chapter may add a fourteenth mark to it or take a mark off it, and the tenth reading is still not on it and the occupier has still refused it in her own hand, and the thirteenth return at the node was NINETY-ONE HUNDREDWEIGHT on day 406, which is a different number from the ninety of day 397 and is entered as a different number.**
